# Project Registry

All active and completed projects. Each project has a project code, a macro code,
a JSON file, and a PRD set.

**Project code format:** `{MACRO}_{6CHAR}` (e.g., `ADVI_V1VERS`)
**Thread serials:** `{PROJECT_CODE}-{NNN}` (e.g., `ADVI_YANGV2-001`)
**Project JSON:** `org/projects/{PROJECT_CODE}.json`

Multiple projects can share the same macro code. The macro code identifies
the surface area; the project code identifies the specific initiative.

---

| `OPAL_SQSCHAT` | `OPAL` | Cross-device agent messaging over SQS — laptop fabric ↔ site Z | **OPENED 2026-07-29 (Andrew-direct at the site-Z terminal, verbatim: *"make sure that we have functional SQS messaging across devices"* / *"just frontrun the SQS creation"* / *"run it as a project OPAL_SQSCHAT"*). THE DEFECT, MEASURED: `opal-agent-comms` is a localhost broker and `org/mcp/liveness.json` is gitignored (`.gitignore:237`) — both DEVICE-LOCAL, so a `~Z` session cannot see or address the laptop fabric over MCP. `get_live_agents()` on site Z returned 1 (itself) while CTO/CVO/DT/DE were provably alive on the laptop via 11 pushes in the prior hour. **The fabric had no cross-device liveness oracle and no cross-device delivery channel** — only the git trunk, which is a HISTORY tier (no ack, no ordering, needs a pull). **BUILT:** queue-pair `agentbus-ajbdev-{sitez-to-laptop,laptop-to-sitez}.fifo` (FIFO, content-dedup, SSE, 20s long-poll, 14d retention) + helper `.tools/agentbus-sqs.sh` (send/recv/peek), mirroring the `agentbus-sim01` convention. **PROOF STATE (UPDATED 2026-07-29, both legs now MET): CROSS-DEVICE IS PROVEN IN BOTH DIRECTIONS** — each leg carries an agent-side ACK naming the MessageId **and** a consumer-side receive event, because an ack alone proves the agent read the content, never that this transport delivered it. Offline-durability exercised on real traffic, not a staged control (a message sent while the laptop had no consumer was collected intact ~26 min after re-arm). Queue depth remains producer-side and is still not a delivery oracle (Rule 22b). **Spawn capability on site Z verified end-to-end** (`1.0.0_SA_CTO~Z` registered after ~240s) — after fixing a real defect: `spawn-agent.ps1` passed an unquoted `ajb.ps1` path through `wt.exe`, whose `CommandLineToArgvW` strips quotes, so any profile path containing a space died while the launcher printed success. Harmless on `AndrewBerman`, fatal on `Opal Bloomberg`. Fixed via 8.3 short-path + `Start-Process` fallback. **TRANSPORT DIVERGENCE — RESOLVED, AND THE PREMISE WAS FALSE:** there was never a substitution to bless. `eff96f5079` — the very commit cited as Port 2's ratification — IS the act that changed the transport to SQS; LD-0306's `ratification_note` says so verbatim while its `decision_summary` still described the superseded DynamoDB DRAFT, and nothing marked the summary stale. Two agents independently read the summary and stopped there; a CTO arch-gate formally REFUSING a transport the sovereign had ratified 6h earlier came within one document-read of landing as law. | `TRSB_LEDGER` | `TRSB` | Trade Submission: Executor Authorization Enforcement + Audit-Trail Integrity | **Phase 1+2 — DISPATCHED 2026-05-18 (COO~B decomposition; PRD v0.1 by 1.0.0_SA_CTO~B).** Bootstrap-hardening of the Trade Submission tool per Andrew direct directive 2026-05-18. Two pillars: (A) the executors list must be exactly the users with execute power, enforced server-side — today `trade-workflow-handler` explicitly enforces NO role/group check on approve/reject/execute ("any whitelisted user can execute"), the `executors` group only drives notification email, and actor identity is trusted from the request body (5 holes A1-A5); (B) every trade must carry a complete, immutable, append-only record of all parties + timestamps + securities + every action incl. failed attempts — today state is a destructive overwrite with no event log, `EXECUTED` never durably rests, the execution failure path silently erases the attempt, the compliance JSON snapshot (`json_s3_key`) is never written, and rejected trades fall out of the `by-approver` GSI (8 holes B1-B8). Plus (C) the system-of-record DynamoDB table has no PITR / deletion protection. Target: canonicalize authority on the `trade-users` `groups` list (executors group = execute power, enforced); embedded append-only `audit_events` with a durable `EXECUTION_FAILED` event; immutable `submission.json` compliance snapshot on execute; `EXECUTED` as durable terminal state; `by-approver` GSI repoint; PITR + deletion protection; managed/audited `trade-users` list path; portal role-aware Execution-tab gating. OUT of scope per Andrew: real brokerage/custodian integration, FIX, SES production migration — back-office handoff stays as-is. Decomposed by COO~B into 10 TOs (TO-7 dropped — Andrew defers `trade-users` management, manages the list directly). Backend serial lane AE 0.7.0: TO-0 probe → TO-1 group-auth → TO-2 identity-binding → TO-3 audit_events → TO-4 JSON-snapshot+retire-COMPLETE → TO-5 by-approver GSI repoint → TO-8 /users/me endpoint. Concurrent infra lane AE 0.5.0: TO-6 PITR+deletion-protection. Portal lane RD 0.3.2: TO-8b role-aware gating. TO-9 resubmission-lineage = droppable stretch. Phases 0-2 DISPATCHED (GO). | 0.3, 0.4, 0.7 | None — Phases 0-2 dispatched. TO-8b gated on TO-8 ship; TO-9 gated on Phase 1+2 capacity. by the ka_testing Docker runtime (org/ka_testing). Code produced by runtime agents
was verified and corrected by CTO before deployment but originated autonomously.

---

## Adding a new project

1. Pick the macro code from `org/projects/MACRO_CODES.md` (or add one if the surface area isn't listed)
2. Choose a 6-char project suffix that abbreviates the initiative name
3. Project code = `{MACRO}_{6CHAR}`
4. Create `org/projects/{PROJECT_CODE}.json` (copy any existing project JSON as template)
5. Required fields: `project_code`, `macro_code`, `name`, `description`, `prds` (array),
   `status`, `phase`, `pods`, `blocking`, `created`, `thread_counter`, `threads`
6. Add a row to the Active Projects table above
7. Commit with: `org(1.0.0_SA_CTO): register project {PROJECT_CODE}`

## Closing a project

1. Set `status` to `"complete"` in the JSON
2. Move the row from Active to Completed/Archived in this table
3. PRDs stay in `docs/architecture/` -- do not archive active project PRDs

## Thread serial format

`{PROJECT_CODE}-{NNN}` where NNN is zero-padded to 3 digits.
Thread folder: `org/threads/{PROJECT_CODE}-{NNN}-{slug}/`
Receipt in agent folder: `{PROJECT_CODE}-{NNN}-{slug}_{other_serials}.md`

When creating a thread for a project, pass `--project {PROJECT_CODE}` to `create-thread.sh`.
The script auto-assigns the next serial and updates the project JSON.

## PRD location

All active PRDs live in `docs/architecture/`. Pre-org-system (v1 era) architecture docs
are archived to `docs/architecture/archive/` and are not referenced by any active project.
